# TaLogic: opponent AI

715 functions, 438,176 bytes, all byte-matching. Names are the Japanese build's symbols where it has one ([topics/jp-build-symbols.md](../topics/jp-build-symbols.md)); `func_<address>` where it has none. Descriptions are readings of the C; field and parameter meanings are interpretations unless [GAME_DATA.md](../GAME_DATA.md) records evidence. The module's place in the program: [ARCHITECTURE.md](../ARCHITECTURE.md).

Most of the module is one family of decision functions repeated for each computer opponent: 21 sets, each reached from an entry routine `L0nn` ([ai-entries.md](ai-entries.md)) and laid out together in address order, followed by the shared routines from `TaLogic_InitLogicData` (0x275d00) on. A set's own functions carry its number (`TaLogic005_GetSpellPlay`); names that every set repeats carry their address (`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask__00121800`). Most entries describe a function as a copy of another set's one and list the differences; "this opponent's callees" means the copies in the same set. The recurring roles: `GetMinimumTask` takes a unit's task and runs the mission for its purpose codes (`_RunMission`, `_AtkMission`, `_MoveMission`, `_TrapMission`, `_ReviveMission`, `_RevoltMission`, `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_EncMission`, `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_DoNone`); `GetNextActiveUnit` walks the unit lists, calling `CommandUnit` (the "prepare callee", which writes a unit's order) and `GetMinimumTask_Summon` (the "try callee", the summon step); `IsHandPlay`, `GetSpecCardPlay`, `SetComboQue` and `GetSummonPiece` choose a hand card and where to play it; the "999 routine" is the set's `GetSpellPlay` called with the id 999; `GetWarnHideLvl` gives the set's warning level ("hide level"), from the life points and monster count or, in several sets, always 0.

- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask__0010c940`** `0x10c940`, 1248 bytes: `int (list, task, owner)`: side = SzDuel_GetTurnOwner(); this opponent's GetNextActiveUnit below 0 gives 0, as does TaLogic_GetActionUnitFromOwnerID(list, low half) below 0; a high half of 1 resets the record (TaLogic_InitRogicControl, mode 1 when the first purpose is 10) and gives 1. Otherwise CommandUnit, then the first purpose (high byte of wPurpose) through the first switch with first = 1 and, unless a mission was issued, the second purpose (low byte) through the second with first = 0; the result is 1 when a mission was issued, and 0 at once for a second purpose with no case. First switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 14 DoNone; 9 Enc(first, 0, hide level, 2, 1). Second switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 14 DoNone (flag unchanged); 9 Enc(first, 0, hide level, 2, 1). Also: CommandUnit takes (list, record). Case 2 with a negative Atk result resets and returns 1 on the first pass and falls back to Run on the second; case 10 stores 255 and 0 in the result record on a negative step. The third parameter is not read.
- **`TaLogic_CommandUnit__0010ce20`** `0x10ce20`, 608 bytes: As `TaLogic_CommandUnit__001271f0` (0x1271f0) with the set first; stores `TaLogic002_IsActiveMode()` & 0xF into the low nibble of set byte 0x1a1 before the checks; column 0 stores 0x50A/0x5FF or 0xA06 (flags high half kept, low = 1) / 0x6FF (flags = half | 0x10000); kinds from `SzDuel_GetUnitKind` >> 5: 0 calls `TaLogic_CommandEfcMonster__0010d880` or `TaLogic_CommandBtlUnit__0010d490` with (order, 1, nibble) by `TaUtil_IsExMonster`; 1 runs `TaLogic002_CommandMagicUnit`; 2 sends `TaLogic_CommandEncUnit(order, 2, 0, TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__0010eda0(key) & 0xFF)`; 3, 4 call `TaLogic_CommandDefenseUnit(order, 2, 0)`; 5 calls `TaLogic_CommandDefenseUnit(order, 1, 0)`; else 0x5FF. One jump table at 0x2baf50. Callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__0010d080`** `0x10d080`, 1032 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__00118510` (0x118510) with the prepare callee `TaLogic_CommandUnit__0010ce20` (set, entry) and the try callee `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__0010dd50`; a second-list entry is accepted when its `SzDuel_GetUnitKind` answer >> 5 is 3 or 4, the callee being asked again after a non-3 answer. Callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic_CommandBtlUnit__0010d490`** `0x10d490`, 912 bytes: `void (struct DotrTryOrder *order, int flag, unsigned char isActive)`: a copy of `TaLogic007_CommandBtlUnit` (0x12c7f0) whose `TaLogic_IsRootOpen` question always passes 2, whose non-zero-byte loop makes five tries passing 6, and which calls `TaLogic_CommandDefenseUnit` (order, 1, 1) on answer 0 and (order, 1, 0) after the last try.
- **`TaLogic002_GetNearUnit`** `0x10d820`, 84 bytes: Forwarder to `TaLogic_GetNearUnit`: its six stack arguments (word, unsigned halfword, word, word, unsigned byte, word) are passed on as the eight stack arguments unitOwnerIdArray, numberOfValidTargets, allocatedBytes, allocatedBlocks, distance, 0, 2400, ignoreList with the register arguments untouched, and the callee's answer is returned. The register parameters (inferred from the stack offsets), int types and the `TaLogic_GetNearUnit` signature are assumed.
- **`TaLogic_CommandEfcMonster__0010d880`** `0x10d880`, 636 bytes: `void (struct DotrPositionOrder *order, int unused, int deep)`: the same shape: kinds 402 and 403 try `TaLogic_GetNearUnit` twice (key from `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__0010eda0`, limit 2400); kind 417 returns when `TaLogic_CommandGunnerUnit` is 1, else forwards to `TaLogic_CommandBtlUnit__0010d490` with 1 and `deep`; kind 408 first calls `TaLogic_CommandDefenseUnit(order, 0, 0)` when `SzDuel_GetLP` (called with the side in a0) is below 1000, then `TaLogic_CommandBtlUnit__0010d490`; other kinds forward to `TaLogic_CommandBtlUnit__0010d490`. The second argument is never read.
- **`TaLogic_CommandDefenseUnit`** `0x10db00`, 312 bytes: Returns at once when `SzDuel_GetUnit` of the order position is 0; otherwise calls `Ta_GetCurrentSide`, `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` and `SzDuel_GetUnit` again with the same position (results unused), stores kind = 0x3ff when the third argument is 1 and 0x6ff otherwise, then walks `TaLogic002_GetLinePiece(lineType, 0..5)` for lineType 1 or 0..3 for other modes and, at the first position whose `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID` id is negative or equals the order position word, writes that position into the upper half of the +8 word and returns. Flags assumed unsigned char.
- **`TaLogic002_CommandMagicUnit`** `0x10dc40`, 268 bytes: Returns unless `SzDuel_GetUnit` of the order position is non-zero and `Ta_GetUnitEffect` gives an entry; then by the entry's high bits: 13 stores kind 0x5FF; 2 stores kind 0x6FF and puts into the upper half of the flags word the `TaLogic_GetLandChange` cell for the unit's location (6, 2), or the unit's own cell when that is negative. The masked 16-bit land-change result is tested for `< 0`, which can never hold.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__0010dd50`** `0x10dd50`, 612 bytes: The step shape with bound 6; the kind callee `TaLogic_IsHandPlay__0010dfc0` refuses only with -1; kind 0 picks (`TaLogic_SetComboQue__0010e320`), scores (`TaLogic_GetNearCard2` with 3 and 0) and finishes through `TaLogic002_GetSummonPiece` with the score; kind 2 calls `TaLogic_SetSpecialComboQue__0010e540` with mode 0, stores 0 at +4 and returns 1 without the finish; other kinds go through the three-argument pass callee `TaLogic002_GetSpellPlay`. Callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsHandPlay__0010dfc0`** `0x10dfc0`, 856 bytes: The `TaLogic020_IsHandPlay` (0x164680) shape with three-argument last routine `TaLogic002_GetSpellPlay`; after TaLogic002_GetSpecialComboPlay(side, item, unit, 0) < 0 it splits on the first per-side byte (0: unmarked id scan then `TaLogic002_GetSpellPlay` unclamped; otherwise queries TaLogic002_IsMakeDefense(1)/(2) gate an unmarked scan and an early `TaLogic002_GetSpellPlay`, then the marked scan and `TaLogic002_GetSpellPlay` or -1).
- **`TaLogic_SetComboQue__0010e320`** `0x10e320`, 532 bytes: The `TaLogic001_SetComboQue` (0x114d40) best-score walk with the score from `TaLogic_GetEnchantQue2` when `Taku_Get_WinCount()` < 3 and from `TaLogic_GetEnchantQue` otherwise; slot-pick callee `TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__0010e6b0`. Signature assumed.
- **`TaLogic_SetSpecialComboQue__0010e540`** `0x10e540`, 364 bytes: Same opening as `TaLogic_SetSpecialComboQue__0011f3c0` (0x11f3c0); only mode 0 goes on, calling `TaLogic002_GetSpecialComboPlay(side, item, block, 0)` and, when `Taku_Get_WinCount()` is above 3, storing every slot that passes the same tests (427 for 488) in item[8], item[9], ... in turn; only the list count bounds that index. Answers 0.
- **`TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__0010e6b0`** `0x10e6b0`, 468 bytes: As `TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__0011a2f0` (0x11a2f0, sum limit 3500) with one more test directly after the 999 test: an id for which `TaLogic002_IsRefuseEmptyCombo(id)` is nonzero is skipped before its cost is read.
- **`TaLogic002_GetSummonPiece`** `0x10e890`, 1280 bytes: Opponent 002's copy of the summon-position pick. Asks `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` for (side, 0) and drops the answer. Kinds 0, 3 and 4 (+0x3e (kind) >> 5): with an `ownerId`, `TaLogic_IsUnitWin4` is asked once and each free position (not field 8, unoccupied, not a held field 9) is costed with count 2 after resetting the twelve bytes, by `TaLogic_GetInvadeRoot2` when the answer was >= 0, else `TaLogic_GetRoot2`; the lowest reachable non-negative cost wins, none gives -1. With a negative `ownerId` up to six (kind 0, mode 1) or four (mode 2) places from TaLogic002_GetLinePiece are costed with count 7 and the first round with a cost below 14 answers. Other kinds return the last free position without field 8/9 and id. Assumed: int kind (written before read), unit layout as the group.
- **`TaLogic_GetSommonPiecePreference__0010ed90`** `0x10ed90`, 8 bytes: Tail jump to `Ta_GetSommonPiecePreference` (0x24e410) with the arguments unchanged. Two int parameters and an int result assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__0010eda0`** `0x10eda0`, 92 bytes: Same shape as `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__0011fa80` (0x11fa80) with limit 1500 and count 4, but every path returns 0. Signature assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsOpenUnit__0010ee00`** `0x10ee00`, 556 bytes: A verdict (0/1/2) like `TaLogic_IsOpenUnit__0011c140` with a six-entry `switch` on `SzDuel_GetUnitKind(x, y) >> 5` (jump table at 0x2baf70): kind 0 answers 1 for cell words 402/403/427/428, kind 1 is the word walk, kind 5 asks `TaLogic002_IsDoCerem` and `SzDuel_GetField`.
- **`TaLogic002_IsActiveMode`** `0x10f030`, 496 bytes: Fills a 22-halfword stack buffer with 0xffff, calls `TaUtil_GetUnitLst(SzDuel_GetTurnOwner(), 1, buf)`, and returns 1 when any written position (`SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo(word >> 8, word & 0xff)`, low 16 bits) has id 402, 403 or 422. Otherwise returns 0 when id 848 or 798 is found among those positions, among `SzDuel_InqHand(kind, 0..4)` (low 12 bits) or among `SzDuel_InqDeck(kind, j)` for j below `SzDuel_GetDeckCount(kind)` (low 16 bits); else 1.
- **`TaLogic002_IsRefuseEmptyCombo`** `0x10f220`, 108 bytes: `int (int id)`. Returns 0 when `TaHand_GetCardKind()` >> 5 is non-zero; otherwise 1 when `Ta_GetHandEnchant(id, 0x31e)` is not negative or id is 0x18e, else 0. Int callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic002_GetSpecialComboPlay`** `0x10f290`, 440 bytes: Reads the side id list; -1 for a non-zero mode byte, a non-null `SzDuel_GetMapUnit(0, 0)` answer or a `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(side, 0)` answer other than 1; otherwise stores the last slot holding id 798 at minTask[6] and, with one, takes the first id with kind >> 5 of zero, a non-negative `Ta_GetHandEnchant(id, 798)` answer and a `TaHand_IsCardSummon` answer of 1 (minTask[7], `SzDuel_InitCardInfo` mark, answer kind >> 5); -1 otherwise. Assumed: as `TaLogic004_GetSpecialComboPlay`.
- **`TaLogic002_GetSpellPlay`** `0x10f450`, 668 bytes: Picks a hand card to play: fills the side's hand ids (TaHand_GetNextHand), returns -1 for a null unit, sets minTask[6..10] to 0xff, then takes the first card of kind 5 that `TaLogic002_IsDoCerem` accepts, else the first of kind 3 or 4, else the first of a kind other than 0 or 2, else the first of any non-zero kind; the chosen slot goes in minTask[6], the card into the unit (SzDuel_InitCardInfo) and 1 is returned. -1 when none.
- **`TaLogic002_IsMakeDefense`** `0x10f6f0`, 340 bytes: For defenseNo 1 walks `TaLogic002_GetLinePiece` index 0..5 (index 0 and 5 give the same position, so a hit there counts twice), otherwise the four; a position counts when its `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID` id is not negative, the id low byte is nonzero and `Ta_IsUnitControl(id >> 8, low)` equals `SzDuel_GetTurnOwner()`; returns 1 for a count of at least two (defenseNo 1) or at least one (other modes), else 0. Argument assumed unsigned char.
- **`TaLogic002_GetLinePiece`** `0x10f850`, 232 bytes: For lineType 1 returns 0x203, 0x302, 0x301, 0x300, 0x104, 0x203, 4 for `lineIndex` 0..6 and 0x203 otherwise; for any other lineType returns 0x102, 0x201, 0x200, 2 for `lineIndex` 0..3 and 0x102 otherwise. Both arguments assumed unsigned char (masked on entry).
- **`TaLogic002_IsDoCerem`** `0x10f940`, 288 bytes: As `TaLogic019_IsDoCerem` (0x162490, limit 3000) but first reads each listed cell word through `SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo` and returns 0 when it is 402, 403, 427 or 428.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__0010fa60`** `0x10fa60`, 1420 bytes: `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__001336a0` with the side routine expanded in place (`SzDuel_GetLP` above 1500, then `SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount` at least 4; every outcome is 0 as the fifth argument of `TaLogic_GetUnitWarning`), 1 as the fourth argument of `TaLogic_IsDefStyleUnit` on the away pick, tail callee `TaLogic_IsOpenUnit__0010ee00`. Assumes `SzDuel_GetLP` takes the side.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_AtkMission__0010fff0`** `0x10fff0`, 1784 bytes: `(order, side, result, mode)`: attack step for this opponent. -1 with the order reset (`TaLogic_InitRogicControl`, mode 1) when no unit stands at the target spot; otherwise result kind 6 with the unit's own cell when it cannot move (+0x10 is 1 when the cell warning reaches its attack points), with the target cell as soon as an open move to it exists, or with the candidate cell of lowest warning (ties: nearer the target); +0x0c and +0x10 take the open flag and `TaLogic_IsDefStyleUnit`. The unit's GetWarnHideLvl (bound 1500, every path 0) and an open-move test (distance 2, card 417 excluded, status and land agreement) are expanded in place.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_MoveMission__001106f0`** `0x1106f0`, 2064 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_MoveMission__001459e0` (0x1459e0) with IsOpenUnit at `TaLogic_IsOpenUnit__0010ee00`, this opponent's GetWarnHideLvl expanded in place (SzDuel_GetLP over 1500, then monster count 4 or more; every path gives 0) and (2, 1, level) as the last GetUnitWarning arguments.
- **`TaLogicCom_GetRevoltObj__001110d0`** `0x1110d0`, 592 bytes: Returns -1 when `Ta_SearchUncTypeNaturalEffectUserNum(57)` is positive; otherwise scans the 22-halfword list filled by `TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst(side == 0, list, 52, 0)` for entries one step from the `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(side == 0, 0)` cell with zero `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(.., 1)`, `SzDuel_GetUnitCurseRest` and `SzDuel_GetUnitSpellRest` answers, keeps the greatest `TaUtil_GetUnitAP` score of at least 1 (later entry on ties) and returns that entry << 16 | the last kept entry equal to the first entry of the `Ta_RemoveObjFromFlagLst` set (else 0xffff); -1 when none. Assumed: one int argument and an int result.
- **`TaLogicCom_IsOpenUnit__00111320`** `0x111320`, 4256 bytes: Returns 0 when unit (side, pos) has status bit 4; 1 when its card is an Exodia part (file-local helper) and the owner's Exodia tactic and piece count (== 4) agree; otherwise by kind group (SzDuel_GetUnitKind >> 5). Group 5: 2 on action reaction 5, 1 when the ceremony helper agrees and the field under the unit is not 9. Group 1: 2 on action reaction 5 or no effect, else by the effect word (kind = word >> 8, who = bits 6-7, low = bits 0-5): 0x17/0x49/0x13 the use-object helper, 0x18 more than five who-2 units with status bit 4, 2 a land change onto its own location, 4/0x12 a range spell on its row or column, 0xd who 1 (LP <= 1200 when the high half is 4) or low 0x32 with more than three opponent kind-3 units, 0x1f card 0x2dd with a revolt object, 0x20 a slot with curse count 0xff, 0x12 more than two who-1 units, 0x21 the opponent's strongest unit at or above 3000 AP and stronger than the owner's, 0x36 always, 0x25/0x28 attacked now, 0x26 not hand phase, 0x27 summon power below 4. Group 0 (extra monsters only, else 2): effect list 2 opens for kinds 0xc, 0xd, 0xe, 0xf, 0x22, 0x34, 0x3b, 0x43, 0x56 and on counts or curses for 6, 0xb, 0x12, 0x13, 0x17, 0x47, 0x4d, 0x4e, 0x53, 0x54, 0x55, 0x58 (rank >= 6); then effect list 3 (none: 2) for 0x12, 0x33, 0x17, 0xd, 0x23, 0x18, 4, 0x1a, 0x26, 0x1f, 0x3c, 0x46, 0xc, 0x57. Every other case 2.
- **`TaLogicCom_IsUseObjEffect__001123c0`** `0x1123c0`, 568 bytes: Counts to 22 with no effect, then keeps the best `TaUtil_GetUnitAP` score among the `SearchUnitByRange(0, 0, side, sidesToMatch, searchMode, 14)` entries owned by side (`Ta_IsUnitControl`) and among the others; 1 when the others' best exceeds the side's and 3000; else 0 when `sidesToMatch` bit 0 is set and the (1, searchMode, 14) set is not empty; 1 when `sidesToMatch` >> 1 is non-zero and the (2, searchMode, 14) set holds more than 3; else 0. Assumed: three int arguments and an int result.
- **`TaLogicCom_GetUseXYRangeSpell__00112600`** `0x112600`, 592 bytes: Fills a 22-halfword list with 0xffff and switches on the low six bits of the first record word. 29: `TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit` pick of the `TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst2(side == 0, list, 29, 0, 0)` list and, after a refill, of the `TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst(side, list, 29, 1)` list; with both non-negative returns the `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` cell of the first pick when `TaLogic_IsUnitWin(first, second, cell & 0xff, cell >> 8)` is not negative. 30: for x, y in 0..6 counts (in a byte) the `SearchUnitByRange(x, y, side, 1, 52, 18)` entries with a zero `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(.., 4)` answer or a non-zero `SzDuel_GetUnitKind` answer >> 5 and returns y << 8 | x at the first count above 2. -1 otherwise. Assumed: (int, unsigned *) -> int.
- **`TaLogicCom_IsDoCerem__00112850`** `0x112850`, 260 bytes: As `TaLogic009_IsDoCerem` (0x138f90) with the limit read as the unsigned high halfword of `TaHand_GetCardAPDP(record word)` before the `Ta_GetSacrificeUnit` answer is tested, and an unsigned score comparison. Signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogicCom_IsExzParts__00112960`** `0x112960`, 20 bytes: 1 when id is 54..57 (the Exodia pieces), else 0. Int parameter and result assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsTactixExz__00112980`** `0x112980`, 192 bytes: 1 when `SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo(side, 0)` is 58 and `SzData_CheckDeck(side, SzData_GetCurrentDeck(side), id)` is non-negative for id 54, 55, 56, 57 in order, else 0. Int parameter and return assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsExzodia__00112a40`** `0x112a40`, 336 bytes: Returns 0 unless `SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo(side, 0)` is 58; otherwise for k 0..3 walks the eight `Ta_GetSommonPiecePreference(side, i)` entries, and for each whose `SzDuel_GetMapUnit(lo, hi)` item is non-null and whose `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID(lo, hi)` cell is non-negative sets flag k when `SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo(cell >> 8, cell & 0xff)` == `Ta_GetExzodiaPartsCardNo(k)`, `SzDuel_GetUnitCurseRest` and `SzDuel_GetUnitSpellRest` of that pair are 0 and cell >> 8 == side; returns the sum of the four flags. The flags are a stack array the code never clears, so an unset flag holds whatever was there. Int parameter and return assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask__00112b90`** `0x112b90`, 1444 bytes: `int (list, task, owner)`: side = SzDuel_GetTurnOwner(); this opponent's GetNextActiveUnit below 0 gives 0, as does TaLogic_GetActionUnitFromOwnerID(list, low half) below 0; a high half of 1 resets the record (TaLogic_InitRogicControl, mode 1 when the first purpose is 10) and gives 1. Otherwise CommandUnit, then the first purpose (high byte of wPurpose) through the first switch with first = 1 and, unless a mission was issued, the second purpose (low byte) through the second with first = 0; the result is 1 when a mission was issued, and 0 at once for a second purpose with no case. First switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 9 Enc(first, 0, hide level, 5, 1); 11 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_TrapMission__00116b60`; 12 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__001170a0`; 14 DoNone. Second switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 9 Enc(first, 0, hide level, 5, 1); 11 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_TrapMission__00116b60`; 12 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__001170a0`; 14 DoNone (flag unchanged). Also: stores this opponent's IsActiveMode() in the 4-bit member at +417 first and hands that nibble to GetNextActiveUnit and CommandUnit. Case 2 with a negative Atk result resets and returns 1 on the first pass and falls back to Run on the second; case 10 stores 255 and 0 in the result record on a negative step. The third parameter is not read.
- **`TaLogic_CommandUnit__00113140`** `0x113140`, 560 bytes: `int (struct DotrRuleOrder *order, int deep)`: the prepare callee of `TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__00113370`. An unused `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` read, then the rejections of `TaLogic_CommandUnit__00118330` (`SzDuel_GetUnit` zero, `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus` non-zero, position 0xFFFF) and its column-0 stores, except that a `TaLogic_IsAttackedNow` answer other than 1 stores 0xA06 with the low halfword of +8 set to 0x101, or 0x6FF with the high halfword set to 0x101; other columns go by `SzDuel_GetUnitKind` >> 5 (0: `TaLogic001_CommandEfcMonster` or `TaLogic001_CommandBtlUnit` by the `TaUtil_IsExMonster` answer; 1 and 3: `TaLogic001_CommandSpellUnit`; 2: `TaLogic_CommandEncUnit` with `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00117c30(key)` & 0xFF; else 0x5FF). Callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__00113370`** `0x113370`, 1004 bytes: `int (struct DotrRuleSet *set, int side, int key, struct DotrRuleStep *step)`: the four list stages of `TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__00118510` (0x118510) with a fourth parameter; `key` is passed on unread as the second argument of the prepare callee `TaLogic_CommandUnit__00113140` and `step` as the third argument of the try callee `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__00114080`. Parameter types and callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic001_CommandBtlUnit`** `0x113760`, 672 bytes: As `TaLogic_CommandBtlUnit__0011d110` (0x11d110) with `TaLogic_InitIgnoreLst` after the list fill, `TaLogic_IsRootOpen` asked with 2, and N = 5 when the third argument is non-zero, else 2 (key from `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00117c30`, forwarder `TaLogic001_GetNearUnit`, fallback `TaLogic001_CommandInvadeUnit` taking the order and the third argument).
- **`TaLogic001_GetNearUnit`** `0x113a00`, 84 bytes: Copy of `TaLogic002_GetNearUnit` with the constant 3000.
- **`TaLogic001_CommandInvadeUnit`** `0x113a60`, 256 bytes: `void (struct DotrTryOrder *order)`: the fallback of `TaLogic001_CommandBtlUnit` (which passes a second argument this code never reads). Always stores 0x6ff at +4; the word above the +8 halfword is `TaLogic001_GetNextGrassPoint(column, row)` when `CheckLandAgreement2(3, position bytes)` == 1, else 0x0303 / 0x0305 / 0x0503 by the bounds at 3 and the order of column and row.
- **`TaLogic001_GetNextGrassPoint`** `0x113b60`, 72 bytes: `int (int col, int row)`. 0x602 when row >= 5, else 0x206 when col >= 5, else 0x105 when col >= row, else 0x501. Leaf; int parameters assumed.
- **`TaLogic001_CommandEfcMonster`** `0x113bb0`, 792 bytes: `void (struct DotrPositionOrder *order, int deep)`: kind-0 callee of `TaLogic_CommandUnit__00113140` (taken when `TaUtil_IsExMonster` answers 1). Nothing without a `SzDuel_GetUnit` word; then by the `SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo` word: 83 stores 0x3ff and the `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` cell when `TaLogic001_IsUseLordOfDragon(side)` is 1, else 0x5ff; 137 runs i = 1..5 through `TaLogic_GetNearUnitL2` (chaining its previous answer) and `TaLogic_GetNearUnitL3`, storing 0x2ff and the first `TaLogic_GetNearUnitL3` answer of 0 or more; 191 stores 0xbff when `TaLogic_CommandGunnerUnit(order, 0)` is 1; 377 / 390 the same after `TaLogic001_IsUseLifeHourGlass(side)` / `TaLogic001_IsUseBraveHourGlass(side)` answer 1; everything else ends in `TaLogic001_CommandBtlUnit(order, 1, deep)`. Callee signatures and local buffer sizes assumed.

- **`TaLogic018_IsOpenMoveUnit`** `0x15cf30`, 244 bytes: 1 when `|x1 - x2| + |y1 - y2| == 2`, the `SzDuel_GetUnit` word for (side, pos) is nonzero, `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(side, pos, 0)` is zero and `CheckLandAgreement2(SzDuel_GetField(x1, y1), side, pos) == 1`; otherwise 0. Six int parameters and an int result assumed.
- **`TaLogic018_IsDoCerem`** `0x15d030`, 232 bytes: The `TaLogic019_IsDoCerem` (0x162490) walk with limit 0: returns 0 when the `Ta_GetSacrificeUnit` answer is negative or any listed cell scores >= 0 through `TaUtil_GetUnitAP`, else 1.
- **`TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__0015d120`** `0x15d120`, 92 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00117c30` (0x117c30) with other values: 2000 while `SzDuel_GetLP()` <= 1200, else 1500 when `SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount(side)` < 5, else 750.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask__0015d350`** `0x15d350`, 1420 bytes: `int (list, task, owner)`: side = SzDuel_GetTurnOwner(); this opponent's GetNextActiveUnit below 0 gives 0, as does TaLogic_GetActionUnitFromOwnerID(list, low half) below 0; a high half of 1 resets the record (TaLogic_InitRogicControl, mode 1 when the first purpose is 10) and gives 1. Otherwise CommandUnit, then the first purpose (high byte of wPurpose) through the first switch with first = 1 and, unless a mission was issued, the second purpose (low byte) through the second with first = 0; the result is 1 when a mission was issued, and 0 at once for a second purpose with no case. First switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 15 Move(first, 0); 16 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 9 Enc(first, 0, hide level, 5, 1); 13 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RevoltMission__00161ea0`; 12 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__00161bf0`; 14 DoNone. Second switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 15 Move(first, 0); 16 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 9 Enc(first, 0, hide level, 5, 1); 13 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RevoltMission__00161ea0`; 12 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__00161bf0`; 14 DoNone (flag unchanged). Case 2 with a negative Atk result resets and returns 1 on the first pass and falls back to Run on the second; case 10 stores 255 and 0 in the result record on a negative step. The third parameter is not read.
- **`TaLogic_CommandUnit__0015d8e0`** `0x15d8e0`, 520 bytes: The prepare callee of `TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__0015daf0` (0x15daf0); stages described in [`lower_rules.h`](../../include/dotr/ee/lower_rules.h). Order layout, int result and callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__0015daf0`** `0x15daf0`, 992 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__00118510` (0x118510) with the prepare callee `TaLogic_CommandUnit__0015d8e0` and the try callee `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__0015f300`.
- **`TaLogic019_CommandBtlUnit`** `0x15ded0`, 348 bytes: `void (struct DotrPositionOrder *order)`: the `TaLogic022_CommandBtlUnit` (0x16dca0) shape with `TaLogic019_CommandAtkUnit`, `TaLogic019_IsEmptyCntrGuard` and `TaLogic019_CommandLineUnit`; when the first test is not 1, a non-negative `TaLogic019_IsEmptyTrapGuard(hi, lo)` is stored in the high halfword with kind 0x6ff, else `TaLogic019_CommandLineUnit(order, 2)` when `TaLogic019_IsEmptyCntrGuard(hi, lo, 2)` is 1, else `TaLogic019_CommandAtkUnit(order)`.
- **`TaLogic019_CommandAtkUnit`** `0x15e030`, 684 bytes: The shared CommandBtlUnit try shape with N = 5, no flag and no fallback callee; answer 0 stores 0x3ff like answer 2, and after five other answers it stores 0x6ff and a word chosen by the two `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` rows. One pointer argument and no result assumed.
- **`TaLogic019_IsEmptyTrapGuard`** `0x15e2e0`, 352 bytes: `int (int a, int b)`: takes side = `Ta_IsUnitControl(a, b)`; when `SearchUnitByRange(0, 0, side, 1, 0x33b001c, 14)` is non-zero reads the `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID(0, 5)` cell and returns 0x500 when it is negative, equals (a << 8) | b, or its `Ta_IsUnitControl(hi, lo)` answer differs from `Ta_IsUnitControl(a, b)`; then the same with key 0x2ed001c, cell `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID(6, 3)` and answer 0x306; otherwise -1.
- **`TaLogic019_IsEmptyCntrGuard`** `0x15e440`, 212 bytes: `int (int side, int pos, unsigned char c)`: reads `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID(i, row)` for i 1..5, row 3 when c is 1 and 4 otherwise; skips zero cells, returns 1 for a cell equal to (side << 8) | pos, 0 for a cell whose `Ta_IsUnitControl(cell >> 8, cell & 0xff)` equals `Ta_IsUnitControl(side, pos)`, else 1. `Ta_IsUnitControl` is called with two arguments here.
- **`TaLogic019_CommandLineUnit`** `0x15e520`, 972 bytes: `void (struct DotrTryOrder *order, unsigned char deep)`: the same code as `TaLogic022_CommandLineUnit` (0x16e0e0) with `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00162580` and the forwarder `TaLogic019_GetNearUnit`.
- **`TaLogic019_GetNearUnit`** `0x15e8f0`, 84 bytes: Copy of `TaLogic002_GetNearUnit` with the constant 2500.
- **`TaLogic019_CommandEfcMonster`** `0x15e950`, 776 bytes: Reads the unit controller, card number, unit word and location, clears a 22-cell list and the ignore list, then by card: 43 with status bit 4 clear sets kind 0xcff and the location in the upper half of the flags word; 87 calls TaLogic019_CommandAtkUnit; 297 scans row 3 columns 1-5 (stops with "open" at the unit itself, "blocked" at a unit of the same controller), calls TaLogic019_CommandLineUnit(order, 1) when open, else runs up to two TaLogic_GetNearUnitL2/L3 passes (kind 0x2ff and the answer in the flags word on success) and falls to TaLogic019_CommandLDGuardUnit; every other case calls TaLogic019_CommandBtlUnit.
- **`TaLogic019_CommandLDGuardUnit`** `0x15ec60`, 208 bytes: Calls `Ta_IsUnitControl(row, column)` and drops the result, writes the cell `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(row, column)` as kind 0x3ff with the cell in the upper flag half (the same two stores in both arms of the `TaLogic019_IsLinePoint(cell & 0xff, cell >> 8)` == 1 test), then a non-negative `TaLogic019_GetEmptyLine()` replaces the cell, else `TaLogic019_CommandBtlUnit(order)` runs. Int coordinates, no result and the order type assumed.
- **`TaLogic019_IsLinePoint`** `0x15ed30`, 92 bytes: `int (int column, int row)`. As `TaLogic013_IsDefensePoint` (0x147e50) over `TaLogic019_GetLine(i)`, i in 0..3.
- **`TaLogic019_GetLine`** `0x15ed90`, 172 bytes: `int (int index)`. Reads `SzDuel_GetTurnOwner()` first, then returns `TaLogic_GetSommonPiecePreference__001604a0(side, 0 / 3 / 4 / 5)` for index 0..3 and -1 otherwise. Assumed: `TaLogic_GetSommonPiecePreference__001604a0` takes two ints.
- **`TaLogic019_GetEmptyLine`** `0x15ee40`, 244 bytes: `int (void)`. For i = 0..3 takes pos = `TaLogic019_GetLine(i)` and, when `SzDuel_GetMapUnit(pos & 0xff, pos >> 8)` is zero, returns `TaLogic_GetSommonPiecePreference__001604a0(SzDuel_GetTurnOwner(), 0 / 3 / 4 / 5)` for that i; -1 when no cell is free.
- **`TaLogic019_CommandSpellUnit`** `0x15ef40`, 948 bytes: Writes kind 0x5ff when `Ta_GetUnitEffect(position, 0)` is null, else dispatches on the byte `SzDuel_GetUnitKind(position)` >> 5 (1: by the word, cases 20, 4, 37, 31; 3 and 4: code 749, then the column-3 scan, `TaLogic019_IsEmptyTrapGuard`, the column-4 scan and `TaLogic_CommandGunnerUnit`; cases listed in [`position_rules.h`](../../include/dotr/ee/position_rules.h)). The two column scans are the body of `TaLogic019_IsEmptyCntrGuard` expanded in place. No result assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__0015f300`** `0x15f300`, 724 bytes: `int (int position, int side, struct DotrStepOut *minTask)`: the `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__001687a0` (0x1687a0) shape, with the score taken after an accepted kind 0 pick or directly for other kinds, word 2 stored at +0xc, `TaLogic_GetNearCard2(block, lo, hi, other side, 3, 31, 2, 2, 0, TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00162580(side))`, a `SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount` bound of 5, and kind 3 calling `TaLogic019_GetSpecialComboPlay(side, minTask, block)` before the finish as kind 2 calls `TaLogic019_SetSpecialComboQue`. As in the other copies, the kind 0 path with a refused pick and an accepting pass callee would use an unassigned score. Callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic019_IsHandPlay`** `0x15f5e0`, 1044 bytes: Pick dispatcher of the `TaLogic013_IsHandPlay` (0x1482f0) shape. Stages: 833 and 853 each gated by TaLogic019_IsDoCerem == 1; 700, 715, 685 and 43 each gated by a one-argument finder answering >= 0 (`TaLogic019_GetUseThunderBolt`, `TaLogic019_IsUseGFK`, `TaLogic019_IsUseRevive`, `TaLogic019_IsUseSaint`); TaLogic019_GetSpecialComboPlay == 1 answers 3; a 796 loop and a 795 loop over the eleven SzDuel_GetUnitCurseRest flags; the id scan (passing over id 297) and the 999 routine. - **`TaLogic019_GetSpecialComboPlay`** `0x15fa00`, 340 bytes: Reads the id list with `TaHand_GetNextHand` and walks the `TaHand_GetNextHandNum(side)` slots twice: the last slot holding 790 goes to item[6], then the last slot holding 297 with `TaHand_IsCardSummon2(side, 297)` == 1 goes to item[7]; -1 when either pass finds no slot, otherwise `SzDuel_InitCardInfo(block, 297)` and 1. - **`TaLogic019_GetSpecCardPlay`** `0x15fb60`, 232 bytes: Byte-identical to `TaLogic001_GetSpecCardPlay` (0x1146c0; same callees).
- **`TaLogic019_GetSpellPlay`** `0x15fc50`, 512 bytes: Byte-identical copy of `TaLogic001_GetSpellPlay`.

- **`TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__00160030`** `0x160030`, 468 bytes: The `TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__0010e6b0` (0x10e6b0) code with veto callee `TaLogic019_IsRefuseEmptyCombo` and sum limit 4000.
- **`TaLogic019_IsRefuseEmptyCombo`** `0x160210`, 92 bytes: 1 when `TaHand_GetCardKind(id) >> 5` is nonzero or `TaHand_IsCardEffect(id) == 1`, otherwise 0. Int id and int return assumed.

- **`TaLogic_GetSommonPiecePreference__001604a0`** `0x1604a0`, 8 bytes: Tail jump to `Ta_GetSommonPiecePreference` (0x24e410) with the arguments unchanged. Two int parameters and an int result assumed.
- **`TaLogic019_GetSummonPiece`** `0x1604b0`, 792 bytes: `int (int side, unsigned char kind, struct DotrFreeBlock *unit, int ownerId)`: the code of `TaLogic018_GetSummonPiece` (0x15af30) with the key callee `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00162580`. Same assumptions.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__001607d0`** `0x1607d0`, 1484 bytes: Copy of `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__00149690` (0x149690) with callees `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00162580(side)` and `TaLogic_IsOpenUnit__001620b0(a, b, order)`; `mode` is unsigned char.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_AtkMission__00160da0`** `0x160da0`, 1496 bytes: The body of `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_AtkMission__00129d00` (0x129d00) with this opponent's callees (TaLogic_IsOpenUnit__001620b0, TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00162580, TaLogic019_IsOpenMoveUnit; the order reset is `TaLogic_InitRogicControl` itself) and 2 as the warning kind in the goal loop.

- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RevoltMission__00161ea0`** `0x161ea0`, 524 bytes: Calls `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(row, column)` of the order position and drops the answer; sets both nibbles of twelve stack bytes to 15 (never read again); keeps `TaLogic019_IsUseRevolt(side)`, replaced by -1 when `TaLogic_GetActionReaction(row, column, 0, 0, 6, 0)` is 5. Non-negative answer with a high halfword other than 0xffff: counts non-zero `SzDuel_GetUnit(side == 0 ? 1 : 0, i)` for i = 1.. up to that halfword, at most 10; stores position at result+0, 13 at +2, the `TaLogic_IsDefStyleUnit(row, column, halfword +4, 1, 0)` byte at +16, 1 at +12, count - 1 at +4; `TaLogic_InitRogicControl(order, mode)`; 0. Two int arguments assumed.
- **`TaLogic019_IsOpenMoveUnit`** `0x162390`, 244 bytes: 1 when `|x1 - x2| + |y1 - y2| == 2`, the `SzDuel_GetUnit` word for (side, pos) is nonzero, `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(side, pos, 0)` is zero and `CheckLandAgreement2(SzDuel_GetField(x1, y1), side, pos) == 1`; otherwise 0. Six int parameters and an int result assumed.
- **`TaLogic019_IsDoCerem`** `0x162490`, 232 bytes: After the `TaLogic001_IsDoCerem` steps returns 0 for a negative `Ta_GetSacrificeUnit` answer; otherwise walks the `Ta_GetUnitNumFrom22bitFlagList(answer)` entries of `Ta_GetUnitOwnerIDFromLstCount(answer, i)`, splits each into (entry >> 8, entry & 0xff), asks `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` for that pair and returns 0 as soon as `TaUtil_GetUnitAP(hi, lo, answer & 0xff, answer >> 8)` is >= 3000; else 1. - **`TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00162580`** `0x162580`, 92 bytes: Returns 2000 when `SzDuel_GetLP()` <= 1200 (`SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount` not called), otherwise 500 when `SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount(side)` >= 4 and 1000 below that. One int parameter and int result assumed.
- **`TaLogic019_GetUseThunderBolt`** `0x1625e0`, 264 bytes: `int (int side)`: takes the strongest unit (TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit) of the other side's control list (TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst2, kind 29) and of this side's (TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst, kind 29), each in a 22-halfword list; when both exist and `TaLogic_IsUnitWin` of ours against theirs at their location is negative, returns their unit, else -1.
- **`TaLogic019_IsUseGFK`** `0x1626f0`, 224 bytes: Returns 1 at once when `TaLogic_IsAttackedNow(side, 0, 0)` is 1; otherwise takes the list `SearchUnitByRange(0, 0, side, 2, 52, 14)`, counts (in an unsigned char) the entries whose `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(entry >> 8, entry & 0xff, 4)` is 0, rereading the length from `Ta_GetUnitNumFrom22bitFlagList` on every pass, and returns 1 for a count of five or more, else 0.
- **`TaLogic019_IsUseRevive`** `0x1627d0`, 664 bytes: Returns -1 when `SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount(side)` is 5 or more or when none of `TaLogic_GetGraveCardCount(side, 3, 60 / 87 / 43)` is positive; otherwise searches for id 87, then 43, then 60, each first among the side's `SzDuel_InqGrave` entries below the `SzDuel_GetGraveCount` count and then the other side's (side == 0), and returns owner << 16 | position of the first hit, else -1. Assumed: (int) -> int.
- **`TaLogic019_IsUseSaint`** `0x162a70`, 664 bytes: As `TaLogic019_IsUseRevive` (0x1627d0) with gate `SzDuel_GetUnitSpellCount`, presence ids 700, 715, 827 and search order 827, 700, 715. The argument and result of `SzDuel_GetUnitSpellCount` are assumed int.
- **`TaLogic019_IsUseRevolt`** `0x162d10`, 592 bytes: Same body as `TaLogicCom_GetRevoltObj__0016b3c0` (0x16b3c0): -1 when effect 57 is active, otherwise scans the opposing control list for the adjacent free unit with the highest AP and returns it packed with the flagged-list match.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask__00163130`** `0x163130`, 1320 bytes: `int (list, task, owner)`: side = SzDuel_GetTurnOwner(); this opponent's GetNextActiveUnit below 0 gives 0, as does TaLogic_GetActionUnitFromOwnerID(list, low half) below 0; a high half of 1 resets the record (TaLogic_InitRogicControl, mode 1 when the first purpose is 10) and gives 1. Otherwise CommandUnit, then the first purpose (high byte of wPurpose) through the first switch with first = 1 and, unless a mission was issued, the second purpose (low byte) through the second with first = 0; the result is 1 when a mission was issued, and 0 at once for a second purpose with no case. First switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 9 Enc(first, 1, hide level, 5, 1); 12 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__00166d10`; 14 DoNone. Second switch: 5 Run; 2 Atk; 10 step; 6 Move(first, 0); 3 Move(first, 1); 9 Enc(first, 1, hide level, 5, 1); 12 `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__00166d10`; 14 DoNone (flag unchanged). Case 2 with a negative Atk result resets and returns 1 on the first pass and falls back to Run on the second; case 10 stores 255 and 0 in the result record on a negative step. The third parameter is not read.
- **`TaLogic_CommandUnit__00163660`** `0x163660`, 524 bytes: The one-argument `TaLogic_CommandUnit__00113140` (0x113140) shape; rejects (0) without a `SzDuel_GetUnit` word, with `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus` bit 0 or position 0xffff; column 0 with flag word -1 stores 0x50a / 0x5ff (`TaLogic_IsAttackedNow(.., 1)` == 1) or 0xa06 with low half 0x103 / 0x6ff with high half 0x103; otherwise the `SzDuel_GetUnitKind` kind >> 5 selects `TaLogic020_CommandEfcMonster` or `TaLogic020_CommandBtlUnit(order, 1)` by `TaUtil_IsExMonster` (0, 5), `TaLogic020_CommandSpellUnit` (1, 3, 4), `TaLogic_CommandEncUnit(order, 5, 1, TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__001676d0(key) & 0xff)` (2), else stores 0x5ff; returns 1. - **`TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__00163870`** `0x163870`, 992 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetNextActiveUnit__00118510` (0x118510) with the prepare callee `TaLogic_CommandUnit__00163660` and the try callee `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__001643f0`.
- **`TaLogic020_CommandBtlUnit`** `0x163c50`, 616 bytes: The shared CommandBtlUnit shape (as `TaLogic_CommandBtlUnit__0011d110`) with N = 5, with `TaLogic_InitIgnoreLst` before the list fill.
- **`TaLogic020_GetNearUnit`** `0x163ec0`, 88 bytes: Copy of `TaLogic002_GetNearUnit` that passes the flag 1 instead of 0 and the constant 3000.
- **`TaLogic020_CommandInvadeUnit`** `0x163f20`, 100 bytes: `void (struct DotrPositionOrder *order)`. Calls `Ta_IsUnitControl(position >> 8, position & 0xff)` and discards the result, takes cell = `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` of the same pair, stores 0x6ff at +4 and writes the word at +8 as its halfword OR (`TaLogic020_GetNextGrassPoint(cell & 0xff, cell >> 8)` << 16). - **`TaLogic020_GetNextGrassPoint`** `0x163f90`, 132 bytes: `int (int a, int b)`. For b == 0: 0x106 for a >= 3, else 0x100. Otherwise b <= 5 (negative b included): 0x605 for a >= 3, else 0x601. Above: 0x601 for a >= 3, else 0x605.
- **`TaLogic020_CommandEfcMonster`** `0x164020`, 568 bytes: The `TaLogic001_CommandEfcMonster` (0x113bb0) shape with codes 297 (five-pass `TaLogic_GetNearUnitL2`/`TaLogic_GetNearUnitL3` loop, seventh/ninth arguments 1, key `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__001676d0(side)`) and 79 (kind 0x2ff with the cell when `SzDuel_GetLP(side)` < 2000, then falls through); ends in `TaLogic020_CommandBtlUnit(order, 1)`.
- **`TaLogic020_CommandSpellUnit`** `0x164260`, 244 bytes: Returns at once when `SzDuel_GetUnit` of the position is null; else calls `Ta_GetCurrentSide` and `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` (results unused), stores 0xcff at +4 when the record's +0x3c halfword is 688 and 0x3ff otherwise, and for the first of `TaLogic020_GetLinePiece(0..4)` whose `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID` answer is the order position or negative writes that spot into the high half of the +8 word. - **`TaLogic020_GetLinePiece`** `0x164360`, 140 bytes: `int (int index)`. On index & 0xff: 0..4 give 0x203, 0x301, 0x305, 0x202, 0x204; anything else 0x203.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__001643f0`** `0x1643f0`, 644 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_Summon__00114080` (0x114080) with this opponent's callees (kind `TaLogic020_IsHandPlay`, pick `TaLogic020_SetComboQue`, key `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__001676d0`, finish `TaLogic020_GetSummonPiece`, kind-2 callee `TaLogic020_SetSpecialComboQue`, pass `TaLogic020_GetSpellPlay`), `TaLogic_GetNearCard2` arguments (2, 31, 2, 2, 1) and a monster bound of 6.
- **`TaLogic020_IsHandPlay`** `0x164680`, 652 bytes: `int (int side, struct DotrStateBlock *block)`: the shared IsHandPlay shape with `TaLogic020_GetSpellPlay` as the 999 routine; stages `TaLogic020_GetSpecCardPlay` with 842 then 850 (each needs `TaLogic020_IsDoCerem(side, id)` == 1) and `TaLogic020_GetTrapCardPlay`, each returning 2.
- **`TaLogic020_GetSpecCardPlay`** `0x164910`, 232 bytes: Byte-identical to `TaLogic001_GetSpecCardPlay` (0x1146c0; same callees).
- **`TaLogic020_GetTrapCardPlay`** `0x164a00`, 212 bytes: `int (int index, unsigned char *minTask, void *unit)` (`index` is the side; TaLogic020_IsHandPlay passes its `side`): the first card of that side's next hand (TaHand_GetNextHand) whose `SzDuel_Kind2Type(kind >> 5)` is 2 (a trap): stores its slot in minTask[6], loads it into the unit (SzDuel_InitCardInfo) and returns its kind >> 5; -1 when none.
