Shared head (null unit -1; first count 5: spell pick 1/-1; second count 5: first summonable monster stored, 0). Then per hand card of kind 5: the card record (TaHand_GetCardEffect) gives the id for Ta_GetCeremSacrifice and TaHand_GetCardAPDP; if Ta_GetSacrificeUnit finds a list and every listed unit has TaUtil_GetUnitAP below the upper half word of that value (unsigned compare), the card pick is tried and 2 returned on success. Then ids 700 and 702 through `TaLogicCom_GetUseXYRangeSpell__0016cbb0` and their pick; then for each of slots 0..10 with SzDuel_GetUnitCurseRest non-zero pick 796, and in a second pass pick 795; then the shared summonable scan (stores the id) and spell-pick tail.
- **`TaLogic022_GetSpecCardPlay`** `0x16fc00`, 232 bytes: Byte-identical to `TaLogic001_GetSpecCardPlay` (0x1146c0; same callees).
- **`TaLogic022_GetSpellPlay`** `0x16fcf0`, 512 bytes: Byte-identical copy of `TaLogic001_GetSpellPlay`.
- **`TaLogic022_SetComboQue`** `0x16fef0`, 476 bytes: The `TaLogic001_SetComboQue` (0x114d40) code with slot-pick callee `TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__001700d0`; no other difference.
- **`TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__001700d0`** `0x1700d0`, 468 bytes: The same code as `TaLogic_ConvEmptyCombo__0010e6b0` (0x10e6b0) calling `TaLogic022_IsRefuseEmptyCombo` instead of `TaLogic002_IsRefuseEmptyCombo`, sum limit 4000.
- **`TaLogic022_IsRefuseEmptyCombo`** `0x1702b0`, 304 bytes: 1 when `TaHand_GetCardKind(id) >> 5` is nonzero or `TaHand_IsCardEffect(id) == 1`; for ids 54-57 (unsigned range test compared with 1) also 1 when `SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo(side, 0) == 58` and `SzData_CheckDeck(side, SzData_GetCurrentDeck(side), n) >= 0` for n = 54, 55, 56, 57 in that order, with `side = SzDuel_GetTurnOwner()`; otherwise 0. Return type int assumed.
- **`TaLogic022_SetSpecialComboQue`** `0x1703e0`, 552 bytes: Sets `task[6..10]` to 0xff, reads the side's five ids through `TaHand_GetNextHand` and returns -1 for a null unit object. Otherwise, for each slot below the `TaHand_GetNextHandNum` count that is neither `task[6]` nor `task[7]`, whose id equals `unit->id_3c` and for which `TaHand_IsCardSummon2(side, id) == 1`, `task[6]` becomes the slot (the last one wins). When `TaHand_GetCardKind(ids[task[6]]) >> 5` is 0, each slot that is neither `task[6]` nor `task[7]`, with `Ta_GetHandEnchant(ids[task[6]], id) >= 0`, kind 2 and an id other than 795 and 796 is stored at `task[6 + n]` (n from 1, kept as a byte) and `(short)TaEnc_GetEncScore(id)` is added to the halfwords at +0x38 and +0x3a (two calls). Returns 1. When no slot matches, the code indexes the id array with 0xff, outside the five words. Signature, task bytes and unit layout assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetSommonPiecePreference__00170610`** `0x170610`, 8 bytes: Tail jump to `Ta_GetSommonPiecePreference` (0x24e410) with the arguments unchanged. Two int parameters and an int result assumed.
- **`TaLogic022_GetSummonPiece`** `0x170620`, 792 bytes: `int (int side, unsigned char kind, struct DotrFreeBlock *unit, int ownerId)`: the code of `TaLogic018_GetSummonPiece` (0x15af30) with the key callee `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00172310`. Same assumptions.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__00170940`** `0x170940`, 1484 bytes: Copy of `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__00149690` (0x149690) with callees `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00172310(side)` and `TaLogicCom_IsOpenUnit__0016b610(a, b, order)`; `goalId` is unsigned char.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_AtkMission__00170f10`** `0x170f10`, 1496 bytes: The body of `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_AtkMission__00129d00` (0x129d00) with this opponent's callees (TaLogicCom_IsOpenUnit__0016b610, TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00172310, TaLogic022_IsOpenMoveUnit; the order reset is `TaLogic_InitRogicControl` itself) and 2 as the warning kind in the goal loop.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_MoveMission__001714f0`** `0x1714f0`, 2152 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_MoveMission__00137fb0` (0x137fb0) with IsOpenUnit `TaLogicCom_IsOpenUnit__0016b610`, goal-loop GetUnitWarning kind 2 and flag 1 with the level from `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00172310`, TaLogic_IsDefStyleUnit last argument 0; adds the kind-14 exit (a kind-1 or kind-5 unit with status bit 4 has it cleared and answers kind 14).
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__00171d60`** `0x171d60`, 672 bytes: The `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_ReviveMission__00138780` (0x138780) rule driven by the `SzDuel_GetUnitEffect` entry of the order cell (entry 3 for kind 0, else 0) and the finder `TaLogicCom_IsUseRevive(side, bits 6-7, low six bits)`; non-zero kinds add the `SzDuel_GetField == 9` and `TaLogic_GetActionReaction == 5` vetoes and the bit-4 exit; falls back to `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__00170940`.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RevoltMission__00172000`** `0x172000`, 524 bytes: Copy of `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RevoltMission__00161ea0` (0x161ea0) calling `TaLogicCom_GetRevoltObj__0016b3c0` in place of `TaLogic019_IsUseRevolt` and `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__00170940` in place of `TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_RunMission__001607d0`.
- **`TaLogic022_IsOpenMoveUnit`** `0x172210`, 244 bytes: 1 when `|x1 - x2| + |y1 - y2| == 2`, the `SzDuel_GetUnit` word for (side, pos) is nonzero, `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(side, pos, 0)` is zero and `CheckLandAgreement2(SzDuel_GetField(x1, y1), side, pos) == 1`; otherwise 0. Six int parameters and an int result assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00172310`** `0x172310`, 92 bytes: `int (int side)`: 2400 while `SzDuel_GetLP()` <= 1200, else 2000 when `SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount(side)` < 4, else 1000 (the shape of `TaLogic_GetWarnHideLvl__00117c30`, 0x117c30).
- **`TaLogic_InitLogicData`** `0x275d00`, 300 bytes: When TaTuto_IsTuto(side) is 1, hands the byte from SzDuel_GetPlayer(side) and the set to TaTuto_InitTutorial and returns 1. Otherwise returns -1 for a null set, or empties the 22 entries and 32 ids, clears the mode, the low four bits at +0x1a1 and the halfword at +0x1a2, stores side << 8 and SzDuel_GetUnitSummonOrder(side, 0) in the first entry and returns 1.
- **`TaLogic_AdjustLogicData`** `0x275e30`, 76 bytes: Returns 1 when `TaTuto_IsTuto(SzDuel_GetTurnOwner())` is 1; otherwise clears the halfword at +0x1a2 and returns `TaLogic_DoAdjustLogicData(p)`. Int results assumed.
- **`TaLogic_DoAdjustLogicData`** `0x275e80`, 600 bytes: Returns 1 at once for the tutorial side and -1 for a null block; otherwise clears bCount and the 32 wPadQue words, resets every task whose unit is gone or not the turn owner's (wUnit 0xffff, wOrder 255, dwGoal -1, wPurpose 0xffff, bCond 0), refreshes wOrder when SzDuel_GetUnitSummonOrder(side, (index != wOrder)) is nonzero (the second argument is the result of that comparison), assigns each unassigned existing own unit of both sides, sorts and returns 1. Prototype, locals and structures from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitAssign`** `0x2760e0`, 56 bytes: 1 when any of 22 sixteen-byte entries starts with the given halfword, else 0.
- **`TaLogic_DoUnitAssign`** `0x276120`, 176 bytes: Scans 22 16-byte slots for the first whose halfword `+0` is `0xffff`; stores `ownerId` there, `SzDuel_GetUnitSummonOrder(ownerId >> 8, ownerId & 0xff)` as the halfword `+2`, `-1` in the word `+8`, `0xffff` in the halfword `+4`, clears the low two bits of the byte `+0xc` and returns 1; returns 0 when no slot is free. Slot layout, field names and the 16-bit `ownerId` are assumed.
- **`TaLogic_DoSortTaskList`** `0x2761d0`, 184 bytes: For each of the 22 sixteen-byte slots of `taskList` with id 0xffff, moves the next live slot into it (copied as four floats), marks the source id 0xffff, tag 255, word 0xffff_ffff, aux 0xffff and clears the 2-bit flag field.
- **`TaLogic_GetInputQueFromMinimumTask`** `0x276290`, 2244 bytes: Returns -1 for task kinds 255 and 14; otherwise queues pad words into the 16-bit queue at +0x160 (cursor-to-unit column and row steps chosen by the turn owner, then per kind: 6 a confirm with optional words 2/4/8 and the moves to the target in the order TaLogic_GetQueueXY_Move1 picks, 10 the hand-slot walk when SzDuel_InqHandPhase answers, 12 and 13 a fixed triple and counted 0x4000 words), ends with 64, increments the five-bit count at +0x1a0 and returns 1. Parameter and local names from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetQueueXY_Move1`** `0x276b60`, 284 bytes: Reads the unit id at (gX, gZ); returns 1 when the two cells are one step apart; otherwise, when the x distance is exactly 1, returns whether TaLogic_IsPassRoot(nSide, nID, nX, nZ, gX, nZ, &gUnitID) is 1; any other x distance returns 1. Prototype and local name from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetActionUnitFromOwnerID`** `0x276c80`, 52 bytes: Index of the first of 22 entries whose first halfword equals `ownerId`, else -1.
- **`TaLogic_GetUnitWarning`** `0x276cc0`, 500 bytes: Fills a 22-word list with 0xffff, gets the owner unit, asks TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst for the units of the other side (side 1 when Ta_IsUnitControl2(owner) is 0, else 0; kind 29) and, for every listed unit that exists and lies at a Manhattan distance d with 0 < d < bWarnRange from (nX, nZ), adds -TaLogic_IsUnitWin4(owner, unit, bWarnLvl, x, z, fView) / d when that verdict is not positive; returns the sum. Prototype and local names from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_DoNone`** `0x276ec0`, 76 bytes: Writes the pair, tag 6, the low half of `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc` for the pair and a trailing 2 at +0x10.
- **`TaLogic_GetNearCard2`** `0x276f10`, 452 bytes: Returns -1 when SzGame_GetHSyncCount is above 12000; otherwise fills 22 list entries with 0xFFFF/255, initialises the ignore list, and for chase lengths 1..bChase calls TaLogic_GetNearUnitL2 then TaLogic_GetNearUnitL3, returning the first non-negative unit or -1 when the clock passes 12000 or the lengths run out. Prototype and locals from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetNearUnitL2`** `0x2770e0`, 124 bytes: Forwards thirteen arguments to `TaLogic_GetNearUnitPickUpTree` (0x2777f0) as fifteen, inserting two zeros, narrowing the eighth to 16 bits and reordering the stack arguments.
- **`TaLogic_GetNearUnitL3`** `0x277160`, 908 bytes: Scans wUnitNum list entries; an entry counts when its unit exists, its cell distance from (nX, nZ) is at most bChase, its status 2 and 5 match fOpen and fDef, it is of category sKey and 0 < bDist <= bChase. A list distance below the running minimum (7 at first) takes the unit, sets the minimum to the cell distance and the chase bound to bDist; an equal one calls TaLogic_IsUnitWin4 for the candidate (v1) and the holder (v2) and takes the candidate when v1 > 0 and either v2 <= 0 or v1 > v2. Returns the unit word or -1. Prototype and local names from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetFollowNearUnit`** `0x2774f0`, 764 bytes: Scans wUnitNum list entries; an entry counts when its unit exists, its status 2 and 5 match fOpen and fDef, it is of category sKey and 0 < bDist <= bChase. A list distance below the running minimum (7 at first) takes the unit, sets the minimum to the cell distance from (nX, nZ) and the chase bound to bDist; an equal one takes it only when its card has the higher bDeckCost. Returns the unit word or -1. Prototype and five local names from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetNearUnitPickUpTree`** `0x2777f0`, 1124 bytes: Recursive tree builder: a new node lists a unit of the wanted side standing on it (22 entries at most) and stops when the win check is negative, then recurses into cells within the move range that pass the invade-route and visited checks; gives up above 12000 sync counts. Prototype, locals and struct jp_9b50e from the Japanese debug information.
- **`TaLogic_IsAdjustUnitLst`** `0x277c60`, 104 bytes: Scans up to 22 four-byte entries. Each entry whose halfword equals `unitOwnerId` has byte +2 lowered to `level` when larger; the scan continues past matches and stops at a non-matching `0xffff` halfword. It returns whether any entry matched.
- **`TaLogic_GetTrapNearCard`** `0x277cd0`, 1140 bytes: Applies the filters of TaLogic_GetNearUnitL3 and also requires TaLogic_IsJudgeUnitDanger(unit, fView) == 1 together with either TaTrapA_IsTrapAct(tSide, tID, unit, x, z) == 1 or fTrap == 1. On a tie of list distance and minimum each side is valued TaLogic_IsUnitWin2(SzDuel_GetUnit(tSide, tID), unit, x, z) when in danger and bWarnLvl otherwise, and the candidate is taken when its value is lower. Returns the unit word or -1. Prototype and local names from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_IsStatusMatch`** `0x278150`, 96 bytes: `targetStatus` 0 returns whether `actualStatus` is 0, 1 whether it is 1; every other `targetStatus` returns 1.
- **`TaLogic_IsJudgeUnitDanger`** `0x2781b0`, 64 bytes: Returns 1 when `canSeeFaceDown` is nonzero, else whether `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(side, pos, 2)` returns 1.
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitWin`** `0x2781f0`, 92 bytes: Returns `TaLogic_IsUnitWin2(SzDuel_GetUnit(unit1OwnerId >> 8, unit1OwnerId & 0xff), unit2, col, row)`.
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitWin2`** `0x278250`, 452 bytes: `int (struct DotrStateItem *sourceUnit, int position, int col, int row)`. Returns 0 for a null sourceUnit; otherwise the sourceUnit score minus the score of the record at (`position >> 8`, `position & 0xff`). Item score: 0 if `TaUtil_IsVarInfoLeader(sourceUnit) == 1` or `+0x3e >> 5` is nonzero, else `TaUtil_GetUnitDP2(sourceUnit, col, row)` when `+0x40 == col`, `+0x41 == row` and bit 3 of `+0x57` is set, else `TaUtil_GetUnitAP2(sourceUnit, col, row)`; plus `TaLogic_GetActOfs(sourceUnit, SzDuel_GetUnit(hi, lo))`. Other score: 0 if `TaUtil_IsVarInfoLeader(SzDuel_GetUnit(hi, lo)) == 1` or `SzDuel_GetUnitKind(hi, lo) >> 5` is nonzero, else `TaUtil_GetUnitDP(hi, lo, col, row)` when `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(hi, lo, 5)` is nonzero, else `TaUtil_GetUnitAP(hi, lo, col, row)`; plus `TaLogic_GetActOfs(SzDuel_GetUnit(hi, lo), sourceUnit)`. Item type assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitWin3`** `0x278420`, 240 bytes: Returns the unit query minus `base`: zero when `TaUtil_IsVarInfoLeader(unit)` is 1, `TaUtil_GetUnitDP2` when the unit's position bytes equal `(x, y)` and bit 3 of +0x57 is set, otherwise `TaUtil_GetUnitAP2`; on a cell of kind 9 the base becomes `GetCap(1500, base)`.
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitWin4`** `0x278510`, 184 bytes: `int (int unit1, int ownerId, int warnHideLvl, int col, int row, unsigned char canSeeFaceDown)`: `TaLogic_IsUnitWin3(unit1, warnHideLvl, col, row)` when ownerId is -1 or `TaLogic_IsJudgeUnitDanger(ownerId >> 8, ownerId & 0xff, canSeeFaceDown)` is 0, else `TaLogic_IsUnitWin2(unit1, ownerId, col, row)`.
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitWin5`** `0x2785d0`, 540 bytes: Null unit 0; position -1 forwards to `TaLogic_IsUnitWin3`; a zero `TaLogic_IsJudgeUnitDanger(hi, lo, skip)` answer compares the plain `TaUtil_GetUnitAP2` / `TaUtil_GetUnitDP2` scores with the argument base, otherwise the gated scores of `TaLogic_IsUnitWin2` (each plus `TaLogic_GetActOfs`) with the other side `TaUtil_GetUnitAP` term plus `TaLogic_GetActOfs` (0 when gated); 3 when the `TaUtil_GetUnitDP2` score is below the `TaUtil_GetUnitAP2` score and that is >= base, 2 when not below and above the base, else 0. Byte-wide skip assumed from its masking.
- **`TaLogic_GetActOfs`** `0x2787f0`, 152 bytes: Returns the halfword of whichever item's kind-27 entry names the other item's low five bits while that item's upper three bits are clear.
- **`TaLogic_GetUnitNum`** `0x278890`, 52 bytes: `SzDuel_GetUnitSpellCount(side)` when the second argument is 1, else `SzDuel_GetUnitMonsterCount(side)`.
- **`TaLogic_TreeAloc`** `0x2788d0`, 96 bytes: Returns null when `*allocedBytes + 56` exceeds `0x10000`; otherwise the address `0x1ed0000 + 24 × *allocedBlocks`, after incrementing `*allocedBlocks` and adding 24 to `*allocedBytes`. The base is an instruction literal.
- **`TaLogic_InitTree`** `0x278930`, 180 bytes: Initialises a 24-byte node (id, five bytes of 255, four null links) and stores `handIndex` at byte 0 or, after copying `source` with `TaLogic_CopyArray`, at the index `TaLogic_GetArrayLength(source)` returns.
- **`TaLogic_GetHandFusion`** `0x2789f0`, 148 bytes: Narrows both to halfwords and returns 999 if either is 999. Otherwise, when `Ta_GetHandEnchant(cardNo, equipNo)` has no bits above the low 16 and `TaEnc_IsEncDo(equipNo)` is 1, it returns that first result; else the low 16 bits of `Fusion(cardNo, equipNo)`.
- **`TaLogic_GetFusionTree`** `0x278a90`, 496 bytes: Reads the next hand; with a combo list, returns 0 unless card nOrder can be summoned and fuses with the root (TaLogic_GetHandFusion not 999); allocates a node (0 when none is left), initialises it with the root number, list and order, returns it when its list has five entries, and otherwise recurses for each hand slot not yet in the list (unless the root is 999), storing each result in the next link slot and advancing only on a non-null result. Recursive; p_fTree is not read. Prototype, locals and struct TreeTag from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetFusionTree2`** `0x278c80`, 156 bytes: Calls `TaHand_GetNextHand(a, stack buffer)`, returns null if `e` is nonzero or `TaLogic_TreeAloc(used, count)` is null, and otherwise calls `TaLogic_InitTree(record, halfword d, e, c)` and returns the record.
- **`TaLogic_AddEmptyCombo`** `0x278d20`, 100 bytes: Shifts four bytes of `combo` up by one and stores `newCard` first, unless `TaLogic_GetArrayLength(combo)` returns 5.
- **`TaLogic_GetArrayLength`** `0x278d90`, 60 bytes: `int (unsigned char *array)`: the number of leading bytes (at most five) before the first 255.
- **`TaLogic_CopyArray`** `0x278dd0`, 40 bytes: Copy five bytes from the first argument to the second.
- **`TaLogic_IsUseHandInCombo`** `0x278e00`, 60 bytes: 1 when any of five bytes equals the given byte, else 0.
- **`TaLogic_GetFusionRes`** `0x278e40`, 208 bytes: Returns the node of the tree whose id has the largest high half of `TaHand_GetCardAPDP`.
- **`TaLogic_GetEnchantQue`** `0x278f10`, 536 bytes: Takes AP (high half) and DP (low half) of TaHand_GetCardAPDP(nRoot) and the next hand; returns 0 when trap 43 is active with status bit 4. For each hand card not used in the combo, of kind class 2, not 795/796, with Ta_GetHandEnchant high half 1 and TaEnc_IsEncDo 1: with a keep list asks TaLogic_IsKeepEnchant for each of nKeepDepth entries, otherwise appends the hand index to nComboLink and adds TaEnc_GetEncScore to AP and DP. Returns AP << 16 | DP & 0xffff.
- **`TaLogic_GetEnchantQue2`** `0x279130`, 64 bytes: Calls `TaHand_GetCardAPDP(*id)` twice and joins the upper half of the first result with the lower half of the second.
- **`TaLogic_IsKeepEnchant`** `0x279170`, 188 bytes: Returns 1 for a null rule or a different identifier halfword. For kind 36 it returns 0 when the upper half of `TaHand_GetCardAPDP(fusionResult)` is below word +4, for kind 37 when it is above; other kinds return 0.
- **`TaLogic_GetRoot`** `0x279230`, 132 bytes: Forwarder: `TaLogic_GetRoot2(SzDuel_GetUnit(a, b), c, d, e, f, g, h, i)`, the last argument a byte.
- **`TaLogic_GetRoot2`** `0x2792c0`, 532 bytes: Returns -1 without unit information or when SzGame_GetHSyncCount is above 12000, 0 when GetMoveRev reports no move from (nX, nZ), -1 when the goal is more than bAbort cells away (|dX| + |dZ|); otherwise grows the tree with TaLogic_GetRootTree2 one distance at a time while EndCond is 0 and the distance is within bAbort, returning the distance after TaLogic_GetShortRoot when EndCond becomes 1, and -1 when TaLogic_IsConnectRoot is not 1, the clock runs out or the loop ends. Prototype and locals from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetRootTree2`** `0x2794e0`, 868 bytes: Reads the move range (GetMoveRev), returns 0 without variable information; for a null node refuses a depth above the limit, allocates (TaLogic_RootTreeAloc) and fills the node (TaLogic_SetRootTreeDat) and stops with *found = 1 at the goal; then scans the cells within range & 15 (window capped to 0..6, Manhattan distance 1..range) and recurses at depth + 1 into each cell that passes TaLogic_IsPassRoot2 and is not on the path (TaLogic_IsRootPast2), advancing the child index only after a child call; returns the node.
- **`TaLogic_IsPassRoot`** `0x279850`, 112 bytes: Forwarder: `TaLogic_IsPassRoot2(SzDuel_GetUnit(side, pos), col, row, destCol, destRow, atkTargetPtr)`.
- **`TaLogic_IsPassRoot2`** `0x2798c0`, 892 bytes: Reads GetMoveRev first, returns 0 for a null unit, 1 for the same cell; for an adjacent goal 0 when the field is 8 without move flag 0x10, or 9 with Ta_IsVirus_Chk3 == 1, or the goal holds a unit other than *gUnitID, else 1; for a goal two cells away it recurses over the middle cell (straight) or over either corner (diagonal); farther goals give 0. Name, prototype and the local dwMoveRev from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_IsRunPassRoot`** `0x279c40`, 128 bytes: Forwarder: `TaLogic_IsRunPassRoot2(SzDuel_GetUnit(a, b), c, d, e, f, g, h)`.
- **`TaLogic_IsRunPassRoot2`** `0x279cc0`, 968 bytes: The same test as TaLogic_IsPassRoot2 except for a unit on an adjacent goal: 1 when it is *gUnitID, 0 when fFoot is 0, otherwise 1 only if Ta_IsUnitControl(RootUnit >> 8, RootUnit & 0xFF) equals Ta_IsUnitControl2(pVarInfo); the two-cell arms call TaLogic_IsPassRoot2 (not itself), so fFoot does not reach them. Name, prototype and the locals dwMoveRev and RootUnit from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetInvadeRoot`** `0x27a090`, 148 bytes: Returns `TaLogic_GetInvadeRoot2(SzDuel_GetUnit(side, pos), c, d, e, f, g, h, i, j, k)`; `i` is read from the stack as a word, `j` as a byte and `k` as a halfword. Argument types otherwise assumed int.
- **`TaLogic_GetInvadeRoot2`** `0x27a130`, 556 bytes: As TaLogic_GetRoot2 with TaLogic_GetInvadeRootTree2 (extra fView and bWarnLvl arguments), but the clock is tested before the unit information, the ignore list is initialised after the distance test, and the loop continues while EndCond is not 1. Prototype and locals from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetInvadeRootTree2`** `0x27a360`, 932 bytes: Reads the move range (GetMoveRev), returns 0 without variable information; for a null node it refuses a depth above bDist, allocates (TaLogic_RootTreeAloc) and fills the node (TaLogic_SetRootTreeDat) and stops with *EndCond = 1 at the goal; then for each cell of the window nX/nZ +- (range & 15) (low bound GetCap(0, ...), high bound capped to 6) on the 7 x 7 board at Manhattan distance 1..range it takes the next child slot (+4 per cell, used or not) and, when TaLogic_IsPassInvadeRoot2 (fView, Node0 = depth is 0, bWarnLvl) returns 1 and TaLogic_IsRootPast2 returns 0, recurses at depth + 1; returns the node.
- **`TaLogic_IsPassInvadeRoot2`** `0x27a710`, 1216 bytes: The same null, distance and field tests as TaLogic_IsPassRoot2; for a unit on an adjacent goal 1 when gUnitID is not null and names it; when Ta_IsUnitControl of that unit differs from Ta_IsUnitControl2(pVarInfo), 1 if TaLogic_IsUnitWin2(pVarInfo, RootUnit, gX, gZ) (when TaLogic_IsJudgeUnitDanger(hi, lo, fView) is nonzero) or else TaLogic_IsUnitWin3(pVarInfo, bWarnLvl, gX, gZ) is positive, otherwise 2; when controlled alike, 0 if the ID low byte is 0 or Node0 is 1, else 1; two-cell goals recurse through the midpoint or either corner with all arguments. Name, prototype and the locals dwMoveRev and RootUnit from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_IsConnectRoot`** `0x27abd0`, 152 bytes: Recursive search over the twelve links at `tree->+4`: 0 for null `tree`, 1 when `currentDistance == targetDistance` (bytes), else 1 as soon as a child call with `currentDistance + 1` returns 1 (the callee result is returned, not a constant), else 0.
- **`TaLogic_GetShortRoot`** `0x27ac70`, 64 bytes: Calls `TaLogic_DoGetShortRoot` with a zeroed word, a byte of 255, the first argument (`pathfindingBuffer`) repeated and a trailing zero.
- **`TaLogic_DoGetShortRoot`** `0x27acb0`, 616 bytes: Recursive search of a piece tree for the goal square; a node whose packed square equals (gX,gZ) at a smaller depth than *Dist resets *Count, stores the depth and writes the first step from TaLogic_GetNextPiece to p1stLoc[0] with p1stLoc[1] = 15/15, at equal depth increments *Count and writes pMovePiece[1]; other nodes recurse into the twelve children with bDepth + 1. Prototype, locals and structure layouts from the Japanese build debug information; the `Loc < 0` arms are dead but present in the code.
- **`TaLogic_GetNextPiece`** `0x27af20`, 96 bytes: Recursive. Returns 255 when `depth` (masked to a byte) is 7 or more or `tree->+0x34` is null; returns the byte `tree->+0x00` when the successor has no successor; otherwise calls itself on the successor with `depth + 1`.
- **`TaLogic_SetRootTreeDat`** `0x27af80`, 56 bytes: Fills a route-tree node: byte 0 = (row << 4) | col (the cell), the twelve child links cleared, and `parentTree` (the parent) stored in the word at +0x34.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinGoalCount`** `0x27afc0`, 80 bytes: Counts the entries of the twelve-entry nibble-pair list `pathfinderBuffer` whose low and high nibbles are both other than 15 (the goal cells found).
- **`TaLogic_IsRootPast2`** `0x27b010`, 312 bytes: Walks the +0x34 links for at most `remainingSteps` objects; returns 1 when an object byte (column low nibble, row high nibble) is on (col, row), or lies between / beside the two cells when they are two steps apart; else 0. Argument meanings assumed.
- **`TaLogic_RootTreeAloc`** `0x27b150`, 96 bytes: As `TaLogic_TreeAloc` (0x2788d0) for 56-byte records at the same base.
- **`TaLogic_InitRogicControl`** `0x27b1b0`, 76 bytes: `void (unit, unsigned char goalId)`: goalId 1 sets the high halfword of the word at +8 to 0xffff, goalId 0 the low halfword; other modes do nothing.
- **`TaLogic_IsRootOpen`** `0x27b200`, 132 bytes: Passes `SzDuel_GetUnit(side, pos)` then `c..h` and the stack byte `maxDistance` to `TaLogic_IsRootOpen2` (0x27b290), returning its result.
- **`TaLogic_IsRootOpen2`** `0x27b290`, 204 bytes: Fills twelve nibble pairs with 0xf, calls `TaLogic_GetRoot2` with `(unit, bytes, unitCol, unitRow, enemyUnitCol, enemyUnitRow, w, maxDistance)` where `w` is `0xffff` when `enemyUnitArrayPos == -1` and `(enemyUnitSide << 8) | enemyUnitArrayPos` otherwise, and returns the result only when `TaLogic_GetMinGoalCount(bytes) > 0`, else 0.
- **`TaLogic_GetAtkUnitNow`** `0x27b360`, 448 bytes: Fills a 22-halfword local list with 0xffff, then for each index below TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLstLen(ownerIds) keeps ownerIds[i] when `SzDuel_GetUnit(hi, lo)` is nonzero, SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(hi, lo, 0) is zero, TaLogic_GetActionUnitFromOwnerID(units, word) finds a 16-byte entry and either the high byte of its halfword at +4 is 2 and TaLogic_IsAtkNowToGoal(entry, 1) == 1, or its low byte is 2 and TaLogic_IsAtkNowToGoal(entry, 0) == 1; returns TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit(list) when anything was kept, else -1. The first argument is unread; entry layout assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsAtkNowToGoal`** `0x27b520`, 304 bytes: Looks up the cell at +0 and the mode-selected cell at +8 (useSecondGoal 1: upper half of the word) through `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc`, calls `GetMoveRev` for the first cell and returns 1 only when `TaLogic_IsPassRoot` answers 1.
- **`TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit`** `0x27b650`, 232 bytes: `ownerIdArray[0]` is a count read through `TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLstLen(ownerIdArray)` (re-evaluated each iteration); for entries `ownerIdArray[1..]` accepted by `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(hi, lo, 4)` the score `TaUtil_GetUnitAP(hi, lo, v & 0xff, v >> 8)` (v from `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(hi, lo)`) must be strictly greater than the best so far; returns that halfword, `-1` otherwise.
- **`TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit2`** `0x27b740`, 276 bytes: As `TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit` but an entry is also accepted when `team == Ta_IsUnitControl(hi, lo)`: the listed unit (from index 1) with status bit 4 or controlled by team that has the strictly highest TaUtil_GetUnitAP at its location; -1 when none.
- **`TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit3`** `0x27b860`, 324 bytes: The `TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit2` (0x27b740) scan with a stricter alternative: an entry is accepted when `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(hi, lo, 4)` is non-zero, or when `key == Ta_IsUnitControl(hi, lo)` and `SzDuel_GetUnitCurseRest(hi, lo) == 0` and `SzDuel_GetUnitSpellRest(hi, lo) == 0`; among accepted entries the strictly greatest `TaUtil_GetUnitAP(hi, lo, v & 0xff, v >> 8)` score wins (`v = SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(hi, lo)`).
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitReadyMove`** `0x27b9b0`, 516 bytes: 0 when SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(wUnit >> 8, wUnit & 0xff) is negative or status bit 0 is set; otherwise takes the move word GetMoveRev(unit, x, y) and scans the cells from (x-1, y-1) to (x+1, y+1) clamped to 0..6, answering 1 for the first orthogonal neighbour with no map unit that is not field 8 with bit 4 of the move word clear and not field 9 with Ta_IsVirus_Chk3(unit, i, j) == 1; else 0. Prototype and locals from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_IsAttackedNow`** `0x27bbc0`, 580 bytes: Returns 0 when unit (nSide, nID) does not exist; otherwise fills a 22-entry list with 0xffff, asks TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst (kind 31, fView) for the units of the side that does not control the unit, and returns 1 for the first listed unit whose move reach (GetMoveRev & 0xf) covers the Manhattan distance to the unit, that has a passable route, does not lose (TaLogic_IsUnitWin <= 0), has no curse or spell rest, and for which TaLogic_IsSafeTrap answers 0; else 0. Prototype and local names from the Japanese build debug information (wEnUnit[22]); callee signatures assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsAttackedNow2`** `0x27be10`, 596 bytes: The same scan as TaLogic_IsAttackedNow with the target cell (x, y) given by the caller instead of read from the unit; TaLogic_IsSafeTrap receives x and y as passed. Parameters assumed from the body (no debug information for this one).
- **`TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst`** `0x27c070`, 592 bytes: Picks a slot range by sKind (0x22: slot 0; 0x1f: 0-10; 0x34 and others: 1-10), then for nSide and for the other side appends (side << 8) | slot for each existing unit that Ta_IsUnitControl2 reports as controlled by nSide and that is in danger or has TaLogic_IsUnitCategory == 1; stops at 22 entries.
- **`TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst2`** `0x27c2c0`, 728 bytes: As TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst with an extra range (sKind 0x1d: slots 1-5) and a different filter: a unit in danger is listed only when TaLogic_IsUnitCategory == 1, a unit not in danger only when sCondView == 1.
- **`TaLogic_IsUnitCategory`** `0x27c5a0`, 200 bytes: Kind 34 answers nID == 0, 29 answers 1..5, 30 answers 6..11, 31 always 1, 52 and any other kind answer nID != 0. Name and (nID, sKind) from the Japanese build debug information; a leading argument is passed but unread (its name is assumed).
- **`TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLstLen`** `0x27c670`, 88 bytes: Counts the halfwords below 0xffff among the first 22.
- **`TaLogic_IsPickUp`** `0x27c6d0`, 160 bytes: Splits into hi/lo, queries `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus` bits 0 then 4 and kind `SzDuel_GetUnitKind >> 5` (3/4); returns 0 or 1.
- **`TaLogic_GetLandChange`** `0x27c770`, 728 bytes: For every board cell counts the cells within `radius` (Manhattan distance, window cut at the board edge) whose field is neither `targetFieldType` nor 8; a cell whose count is at least the best so far and above `arg4` asks TaLogic_GetRoot2 for the route from (`col`, `row`) (unit id 0, abort 30) and is kept when the route is shorter than the best (start 14). Returns the kept cell packed the usual way, (y << 8) | x with x and y the first and second SzDuel_GetField arguments (in the C, `(x << 8) | i`, where the loop variable `i` is the first argument), or -1.
- **`TaLogic_CommandEncUnit`** `0x27ca50`, 1476 bytes: Returns 0 when the task unit is gone; otherwise switches on the enchant id of the unit card: gunner command, cursed/AP-ranked target lists picked with TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit2, or a chase search over bChaseRange rings with Do_ChkEncMatch; writes wPurpose and the high half of dwGoal on success. Names, locals and layouts from the Japanese build debug information.
- **`TaLogic_GetMinimumTask_EncMission`** `0x27d020`, 1780 bytes: Task for the enchant order (purpose 9, set by `TaLogic_CommandEncUnit`). Goal cell from the order word (high half in mode 1); if the goal is 0xffff and the unit stands there, or TaLogic_GetInvadeRoot (with 3 and the warning level) leaves no goal cell, or every cell is occupied, the task goes to the unit's own cell; otherwise to the free cell with the lowest TaLogic_GetUnitWarning, ties to the cell nearer the goal. Purpose 6, defence and open both 2.
- **`TaLogic_CommandGunnerUnit`** `0x27d720`, 264 bytes: Side = `Ta_GetCurrentSide` of the order position; fills a 22-halfword list with 0xffff; answers 0 when `SzDuel_GetUnitKind(position)` >> 5 is 0 and `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(position, 4)` is set; otherwise `TaLogic_GetUnitCtlLst2(side == 0, list, 52, 0, 0)`, pick = `TaLogic_GetMaxApUnit3(list, side == 0)`, 0 for a negative pick, else `TaLogic_SetTaskAtkOrMove(order, pick, mode)` and 1. Order layout, int mode and result and the `TaLogic_SetTaskAtkOrMove` signature assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetActionReaction`** `0x27d830`, 524 bytes: By kind byte `n`, 4/5/0 ask Ta_IsTrapBInDecideOperation2 (low half 1: return 5, or 2 for kind 4 with high half 1); kind 0 then takes the unit location and Chk_TrapA with an effect record preset to -1 (eNO 113/45/46 -> 1, 44 -> 2); kind 6 returns 5 when natural effect 14 has a user or the opposing trap B 14 is active and (flag byte 1 or unit status bit 4). Chk_TrapA record layout and prototype from the Japanese build debug information; parameter meanings beyond the names (`side1`, `pos1`, `side2`, `pos2`, `n`, `last`) assumed.
- **`TaLogic_GetMaxAPGraveCard`** `0x27da40`, 380 bytes: Scans list `side` when `mask & 1` and list `side == 0 ? 1 : 0` when `mask >> 1` is non-zero. For each position `i` (count from `SzDuel_GetGraveCount`, id from `SzDuel_InqGrave`) whose `TaHand_GetCardKind` state has `>> 5 == 0`, the score is `(unsigned)TaHand_GetCardAPDP(id) >> 16`; a strictly greater score replaces the best, which starts at -1. It returns `(owner << 24) | (index << 16) | score`, i.e. -1 when nothing qualifies.
- **`TaLogic_GetGraveEntryAfterScreen`** `0x27dbc0`, 340 bytes: Mode byte 31 returns `target & 0xff`; modes 29/30 count the positions before `target` whose `TaHand_GetCardKind(id) >> 5` is zero/non-zero, returned as a byte.
- **`TaLogic_GetGraveCardCount`** `0x27dd20`, 252 bytes: Counts the list entries equal to `id` in list `side` (flags bit 0) and in list `side == 0 ? 1 : 0` (flags >> 1), and returns `count & 0xff`.
- **`TaLogic_GetNearUnit`** `0x27de20`, 808 bytes: Refreshes the near-unit list with TaLogic_GetNearUnitL2 (passing the address of its own wUnitNum argument), then asks TaLogic_GetNearUnitL3 for key 30; if a unit is found and TaUtil_GetUnitAP2 of the caller is at most Powerful it sets wPurpose 0x2ff, stores the unit in the high half of dwGoal and returns 1. Otherwise key 29 and, only if that finds nothing, key 52 (fOpen 0): the found unit goes into dwGoal; TaLogic_IsUnitWin4 > 0 calls TaLogic_SetTaskAtkOrMove and returns 1; else with curse rest below 2 and spell rest 0 it returns TaLogic_IsUnitWin5; otherwise -1. Prototype, locals and structures from the Japanese build debug information; callee prototypes are assumed.
- **`TaLogic_RemoveHideUnit`** `0x27e150`, 184 bytes: `n = Ta_GetUnitNumFrom22bitFlagList(flagList)`, `acc = flagList`; for `i < n`: `v = Ta_GetUnitOwnerIDFromLstCount(flagList, i)`; when `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(v >> 8, v & 0xff, 4)` is 0, `acc = ConvUnitIDtoFlagList_OFF(v >> 8, v & 0xff, acc)`; returns `acc`. The caller passes (hi, lo, acc) where `ConvUnitIDtoFlagList_OFF` is declared `(x, flag, n)`: the names there are provisional.
- **`TaLogic_CommandStdLDUnit`** `0x27e210`, 324 bytes: Code 0x050a/0x05ff when `TaLogic_IsAttackedNow(row, column, c)` is 1 or `TaLogic_IsAttackedNow2(row, column, destinationCol, destinationRow, c)` is non-zero; code 0x060a/0x06ff and word +8 = low halfword | (destinationRow << 8 | destinationCol) << 16 when `TaLogic_IsAttackedNow2` is 0; low byte 0x0a when `SzDuel_InqHandPhase(row)` is 1, else 0xff. Order layout as `struct DotrRuleOrder`.
- **`TaLogic_IsDefStyleUnit`** `0x27e360`, 592 bytes: 2 when `pos` is 0 or word is not `SzDuel_GetUnitLoc(side, pos)`; otherwise reaction = `TaLogic_GetActionReaction(side, pos, word & 0xff, word >> 8, 4, last)` (a second call with 5 is dropped) and the answer depends on `SzDuel_GetUnitStatus(side, pos, 5)`, `SzDuel_GetUnitKind(side, pos)` >> 5, the comparison of `SzDuel_GetUnitAP(side, pos, 0)` with `SzDuel_GetUnitDP(side, pos, 0)`, `isMove3` and whether reaction is 2 or 5 (cases in [`cell_grades.h`](../../include/dotr/ee/cell_grades.h)); under a set bit 5 the three reaction tests give the same answer. Parameter types (unsigned halfword word, unsigned byte isMove3), int result and the `TaLogic_GetActionReaction` signature assumed.
- **`TaLogic_IsSafeTrap`** `0x27e5b0`, 444 bytes: 1 when `Ta_IsActiveTrapB(Ta_IsUnitControl(ownerId >> 8, ownerId & 0xff), 4)` is not negative; otherwise walks x over `GetCap(0, cx - 1)` .. `GetCap(0, cx + 1)` and y likewise around cy (upper bounds re-read every step) and, for a cell at distance |x - cx| + |y - cy| == 1 whose `SzDuel_GetMapUnitID` word is not negative, answers 1 when `SzDuel_GetUnitKind` >> 5 of that word (high byte, low byte) is 3, `SzDuel_GetUnitCardNo` is not 813 and `Ta_IsUnitControl` equals the first answer; 0 otherwise. Three int parameters assumed.
- **`TaLogic_InitIgnoreLst`** `0x27e770`, 8 bytes: Does nothing (an empty function).
- **`TaLogic_SetTaskAtkOrMove`** `0x27e780`, 188 bytes: Looks up the enemy unit's location with SzDuel_GetUnitLoc, asks TaLogic_GetActionReaction for the task unit against that location and stores purpose 0x6ff with the location in the high half of dwGoal for answers 1 and 2, otherwise 0x2ff with the enemy id. Prototype, local names and the task structure (unnamed tag jp_9add8) come from the Japanese build's debug information.
